   490→    def test_import_merges_with_state(self, state_with_findings, sample_findings_data):
   491→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   492→        diff = import_review_findings(sample_findings_data, state_with_findings, "typescript")
   493→        # Original findings should still be there
   494→        assert "unused::src/foo.ts::bar" in state_with_findings["findings"]
   495→        assert diff["new"] == 3
   496→
   497→    def test_import_preserves_wontfix_findings(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data):
   498→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   499→        # First import
   500→        import_review_findings(sample_findings_data, empty_state, "typescript")
   501→        # Mark one as wontfix
   502→        for f in empty_state["findings"].values():
   503→            if "naming_quality" in f["id"]:
   504→                f["status"] = "wontfix"
   505→                f["note"] = "intentionally generic"
   506→                break
   507→        # Second import with same findings
   508→        import_review_findings(sample_findings_data, empty_state, "typescript")
   509→        # Wontfix should NOT be auto-resolved (it's still in current findings)
   510→        wontfix = [f for f in empty_state["findings"].values()
   511→                    if f["status"] == "wontfix"]
   512→        # The finding still exists
   513→        assert any("naming_quality" in f["id"] for f in empty_state["findings"].values())
   514→
   515→    def test_import_sets_lang(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data):
   516→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   517→        import_review_findings(sample_findings_data, empty_state, "python")
   518→        for f in empty_state["findings"].values():
   519→            assert f["lang"] == "python"

   534→    def test_id_collision_different_summaries(self, empty_state):
   535→        """Two findings for same file/dimension/identifier but different summaries
   536→        must both appear in state (#56)."""
   537→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   538→        data = [
   539→            {
   540→                "file": "src/foo.ts",
   541→                "dimension": "naming_quality",
   542→                "identifier": "processData",
   543→                "summary": "processData is vague — rename to reconcileInvoice",
   544→                "evidence_lines": [15],
   545→                "confidence": "high",
   546→            },
   547→            {
   548→                "file": "src/foo.ts",
   549→                "dimension": "naming_quality",
   550→                "identifier": "processData",
   551→                "summary": "processData doesn't indicate the return type",
   552→                "evidence_lines": [15],
   553→                "confidence": "medium",
   554→            },
   555→        ]
   556→        diff = import_review_findings(data, empty_state, "typescript")
   557→        assert diff["new"] == 2
   558→        assert len(empty_state["findings"]) == 2
   559→
   560→    def test_id_stable_for_same_summary(self, empty_state):
   561→        """Same summary should produce the same finding ID (stable hash)."""
   562→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   563→        data = [{
   564→            "file": "src/foo.ts",
   565→            "dimension": "naming_quality",
   566→            "identifier": "processData",
   567→            "summary": "processData is vague",
   568→            "confidence": "high",
   569→        }]
   570→        import_review_findings(data, empty_state, "typescript")
   571→        ids_first = set(empty_state["findings"].keys())
   572→
   573→        # Import again — should match same IDs (no new findings)
   574→        diff = import_review_findings(data, empty_state, "typescript")
   575→        assert diff["new"] == 0
   576→        assert set(empty_state["findings"].keys()) == ids_first

   579→# ── Scoring integration tests ─────────────────────────────────────
   580→
   581→class TestScoringIntegration:
   582→    def test_review_findings_appear_in_scoring(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data):
   583→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   584→        from desloppify.scoring import compute_dimension_scores
   585→        import_review_findings(sample_findings_data, empty_state, "typescript")
   586→
   587→        # With assessment-based scoring, review findings alone don't create
   588→        # dimension penalties. Assessments create first-class scoring dimensions.
   589→        assessments = {"naming_quality": {"score": 75}, "comment_quality": {"score": 85}}
   590→        potentials = {"review": 2}
   591→        dim_scores = compute_dimension_scores(
   592→            empty_state["findings"], potentials,
   593→            review_assessments=assessments)
   594→        assert "Naming Quality" in dim_scores
   595→        assert dim_scores["Naming Quality"]["score"] == 75.0
   596→
   597→    def test_review_findings_not_auto_resolved_by_scan(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data):
   598→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   599→        from desloppify.state import merge_scan
   600→        # Import review findings
   601→        import_review_findings(sample_findings_data, empty_state, "typescript")
   602→        review_ids = {f["id"] for f in empty_state["findings"].values()
   603→                      if f["detector"] == "review"}
   604→
   605→        # Simulate a normal scan with no review detector in potentials
   606→        merge_scan(empty_state, [],
   607→                   lang="typescript",
   608→                   potentials={"unused": 10, "smells": 50})
   609→
   610→        # Review findings should still be open (not auto-resolved)
   611→        for fid in review_ids:
   612→            if fid in empty_state["findings"]:
   613→                assert empty_state["findings"][fid]["status"] == "open"
   614→
   615→    def test_review_in_file_based_detectors(self):
   616→        from desloppify.scoring import _FILE_BASED_DETECTORS
   617→        assert "review" in _FILE_BASED_DETECTORS
   618→
   619→    def test_audit_coverage_dimension_exists(self):
   620→        from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS
   621→        dim_names = [d.name for d in DIMENSIONS]
   622→        assert "Audit coverage" in dim_names
   623→        rc = [d for d in DIMENSIONS if d.name == "Audit coverage"][0]
   624→        assert rc.tier == 4
   625→        assert "subjective_review" in rc.detectors
   626→        assert "review" not in rc.detectors
   627→

   631→class TestAssessmentImport:
   632→    def test_import_new_format_with_assessments(self):
   633→        from desloppify.state import _empty_state
   634→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
   635→        state = _empty_state()
   636→        data = {
   637→            "assessments": {"naming_quality": 75, "comment_quality": 85},
   638→            "findings": [
   639→                {
   640→                    "file": "src/foo.ts",
   641→                    "dimension": "naming_quality",
   642→                    "identifier": "x",
   643→                    "summary": "bad name",
   644→                    "confidence": "high",
   645→                },
   646→            ],
   647→        }
   648→        diff = import_review_findings(data, state, "typescript")
   649→        assert diff["new"] == 1
   650→        assert len(state["findings"]) == 1
   651→        assessments = state["review_assessments"]
   652→        assert "naming_quality" in assessments
   653→        assert assessments["naming_quality"]["score"] == 75
   654→        assert "comment_quality" in assessments
   655→        assert assessments["comment_quality"]["score"] == 85

   675→    def test_holistic_assessment_overwrites_per_file(self):
   676→        from desloppify.state import _empty_state
   677→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings, import_holistic_findings
   678→        state = _empty_state()
   679→        # Import per-file assessments first
   680→        per_file_data = {
   681→            "assessments": {"abstraction_fitness": 60},
   682→            "findings": [],
   683→        }
   684→        import_review_findings(per_file_data, state, "typescript")
   685→        assert state["review_assessments"]["abstraction_fitness"]["score"] == 60
   686→
   687→        # Import holistic assessments for the same dimension with a different score
   688→        holistic_data = {
   689→            "assessments": {"abstraction_fitness": 40},
   690→            "findings": [],
   691→        }
   692→        import_holistic_findings(holistic_data, state, "typescript")
   693→        # Holistic wins
   694→        assert state["review_assessments"]["abstraction_fitness"]["score"] == 40
   695→        assert state["review_assessments"]["abstraction_fitness"]["source"] == "holistic"
   696→
   697→    def test_per_file_does_not_overwrite_holistic(self):
   698→        from desloppify.state import _empty_state

   700→        state = _empty_state()
   701→        # Import holistic first

   703→            "assessments": {"abstraction_fitness": 40},
   704→            "findings": [],
   705→        }
   706→        import_holistic_findings(holistic_data, state, "typescript")
   707→        assert state["review_assessments"]["abstraction_fitness"]["score"] == 40
   708→
   709→        # Import per-file for the same dimension
   710→        per_file_data = {
   711→            "assessments": {"abstraction_fitness": 80},
   712→            "findings": [],
   713→        }
   714→        import_review_findings(per_file_data, state, "typescript")
   715→        # Holistic score should be preserved
   716→        assert state["review_assessments"]["abstraction_fitness"]["score"] == 40
   717→        assert state["review_assessments"]["abstraction_fitness"]["source"] == "holistic"
   718→

   762→# ── Staleness tests ───────────────────────────────────────────────
   763→
   764→class TestStaleness:
   765→    def test_stale_after_max_age(self):
   766→        from desloppify.review import _count_stale, _count_fresh
   767→        old = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=60)).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   768→        state = {
   769→            "review_cache": {
   770→                "files": {
   771→                    "foo.ts": {"content_hash": "abc", "reviewed_at": old, "finding_count": 0},
   772→                }
   773→            }
   774→        }
   775→        assert _count_stale(state, 30) == 1
   776→        assert _count_fresh(state, 30) == 0
   777→
   778→    def test_fresh_within_max_age(self):
   779→        from desloppify.review import _count_stale, _count_fresh
   780→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   781→        state = {
   782→            "review_cache": {
   783→                "files": {
   784→                    "foo.ts": {"content_hash": "abc", "reviewed_at": now, "finding_count": 0},
   785→                }
   786→            }
   787→        }
   788→        assert _count_stale(state, 30) == 0
   789→        assert _count_fresh(state, 30) == 1
   790→
   791→    def test_mixed_fresh_and_stale(self):
   792→        from desloppify.review import _count_stale, _count_fresh
   793→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   794→        old = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=60)).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   795→        state = {
   796→            "review_cache": {
   797→                "files": {
   798→                    "fresh.ts": {"content_hash": "abc", "reviewed_at": now, "finding_count": 0},
   799→                    "stale.ts": {"content_hash": "def", "reviewed_at": old, "finding_count": 1},
   800→                }
   801→            }
   802→        }
   803→        assert _count_fresh(state, 30) == 1
   804→        assert _count_stale(state, 30) == 1
   805→

   974→class TestHashFile:

   977→        f = tmp_path / "test.txt"

   981→        assert h1 == h2

   984→    def test_hash_changes_with_content(self, tmp_path):

   987→        f.write_text("hello")
   988→        h1 = _hash_file(str(f))
   989→        f.write_text("world")
   990→        h2 = _hash_file(str(f))

  1009→    def test_review_import_flag(self):

  1250→# ── Sibling conventions tests ────────────────────────────────────
  1251→
  1252→class TestSiblingConventions:
  1253→    def test_sibling_conventions_populated(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
  1254→        from desloppify.review import build_review_context
  1255→        hooks = tmp_path / "hooks"
  1256→        hooks.mkdir()
  1257→        for i in range(4):
  1258→            (hooks / f"hook{i}.ts").write_text(
  1259→                f"export function useHook{i}() {{}}\n"
  1260→                f"function handleEvent{i}() {{}}\n"
  1261→            )
  1262→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[
  1263→            str(hooks / f"hook{i}.ts") for i in range(4)
  1264→        ])
  1265→        ctx = build_review_context(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
  1266→        assert "hooks/" in ctx.sibling_conventions
  1267→        assert "use" in ctx.sibling_conventions["hooks/"]
  1268→        assert "handle" in ctx.sibling_conventions["hooks/"]
  1269→
  1270→    def test_sibling_conventions_serialized(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
  1271→        from desloppify.review import build_review_context, _serialize_context
  1272→        hooks = tmp_path / "hooks"
  1273→        hooks.mkdir()
  1274→        for i in range(4):
  1275→            (hooks / f"hook{i}.ts").write_text(f"function getData{i}() {{}}\n")
  1276→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[
  1277→            str(hooks / f"hook{i}.ts") for i in range(4)
  1278→        ])
  1279→        ctx = build_review_context(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
  1280→        serialized = _serialize_context(ctx)
  1281→        assert "sibling_conventions" in serialized
  1282→

  1284→# ── File cache integration test ──────────────────────────────────
  1285→
  1286→class TestFileCache:
  1287→    def test_build_context_uses_file_cache(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
  1288→        """build_review_context should enable file cache for performance."""
  1289→        from desloppify.review import build_review_context
  1290→        from desloppify import utils as _utils_mod
  1291→        f = tmp_path / "foo.ts"
  1292→        f.write_text("function getData() {}\nclass Foo {}")
  1293→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[str(f)])
  1294→
  1295→        # Cache should be disabled before and after
  1296→        assert not _utils_mod._cache_enabled
  1297→        build_review_context(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
  1298→        assert not _utils_mod._cache_enabled  # Cleaned up after
  1299→
  1300→    def test_build_context_reentrant_cache(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
  1301→        """build_review_context shouldn't disable cache if caller already enabled it."""
  1302→        from desloppify.review import build_review_context
  1303→        from desloppify import utils as _utils_mod
  1304→        from desloppify.utils import enable_file_cache, disable_file_cache
  1305→        f = tmp_path / "foo.ts"
  1306→        f.write_text("function getData() {}\nclass Foo {}")
  1307→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[str(f)])
  1308→
  1309→        enable_file_cache()
  1310→        try:
  1311→            assert _utils_mod._cache_enabled
  1312→            build_review_context(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
  1313→            assert _utils_mod._cache_enabled  # Still enabled — didn't stomp caller
  1314→        finally:
  1315→            disable_file_cache()
  1316→
  1317→    def test_prepare_caches_across_phases(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
  1318→        """prepare_review should enable cache for context + selection + extraction."""
  1319→        from desloppify.review import prepare_review
  1320→        from desloppify import utils as _utils_mod
  1321→        f = tmp_path / "foo.ts"
  1322→        f.write_text("export function getData() { return 42; }\n" * 25)
  1323→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[str(f)])
  1324→
  1325→        assert not _utils_mod._cache_enabled
  1326→        prepare_review(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
  1327→        assert not _utils_mod._cache_enabled  # Cleaned up after
  1328→
  1329→
  1330→# ── Headline bug fix test ────────────────────────────────────────
  1331→
  1332→class TestHeadlineBugFix:
  1333→    def test_headline_no_typeerror_when_headline_none_with_review_suffix(self):
  1334→        """Regression: None + review_suffix shouldn't TypeError."""
  1335→        from desloppify.narrative.headline import _compute_headline
  1336→        # Force: no security prefix, headline_inner returns None, review_suffix non-empty
  1337→        # stagnation + review findings + conditions that make headline_inner return None
  1338→        result = _compute_headline(
  1339→            "stagnation", {}, {}, None, None,
  1340→            None, None,  # obj_strict=None → headline_inner falls through to None
  1341→            {"open": 0}, [],
  1342→            open_by_detector={"review": 5},
  1343→        )
  1344→        # Should not crash — may return None or a string with review suffix
  1345→        if result is not None:
  1346→            assert isinstance(result, str)
  1347→
  1348→    def test_headline_review_only_no_security_no_inner(self):
  1349→        """When only review_suffix exists, returns it cleanly."""
  1350→        from desloppify.narrative.headline import _compute_headline
  1351→        result = _compute_headline(
  1352→            "stagnation", {}, {}, None, None,
  1353→            None, None,
  1354→            {"open": 0}, [],
  1355→            open_by_detector={"review": 3},
  1356→        )
  1357→        if result is not None:
  1358→            assert "review finding" in result.lower()
  1359→            assert "3" in result
  1360→
  1361→

  1364→class TestCmdReviewPrepare:
  1365→    def test_do_prepare_writes_query_json(self, mock_lang_with_zones, empty_state, tmp_path):
  1366→        from desloppify.commands.review_cmd import _do_prepare, _setup_lang
  1367→        from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock
  1368→        import json
  1369→
  1370→        src = tmp_path / "src"
  1371→        src.mkdir()
  1372→        (src / "foo.ts").write_text("export function foo() {}\n" * 25)
  1373→        (src / "bar.ts").write_text("export function bar() {}\n" * 25)
  1374→        file_list = [str(src / "foo.ts"), str(src / "bar.ts")]
  1375→        mock_lang_with_zones.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=file_list)
  1376→
  1377→        query_output = {}
  1378→        def capture_query(data):
  1379→            query_output.update(data)
  1380→
  1381→        args = MagicMock()
  1382→        args.path = str(tmp_path)
  1383→        args.max_files = 50
  1384→        args.max_age = 30
  1385→        args.refresh = False
  1386→        args.dimensions = None
  1387→
  1388→        with patch("desloppify.commands.review_cmd._setup_lang", return_value=file_list), \
  1389→             patch("desloppify.commands._helpers._write_query", capture_query):
  1390→            _do_prepare(args, empty_state, mock_lang_with_zones, None)
  1391→
  1392→        assert query_output["command"] == "review"
  1393→        assert query_output["total_candidates"] >= 1
  1394→        assert "system_prompt" in query_output
  1395→
  1396→    def test_do_import_saves_state(self, empty_state, tmp_path):

  1407→            }

  1412→        saved = {}

  1422→            _do_import(str(findings_file), empty_state, lang, "fake_sp")
  1423→
  1424→        assert saved["sp"] == "fake_sp"
  1425→        assert len(empty_state["findings"]) == 1

  1497→    def test_setup_builds_dep_graph(self, tmp_path):

  1500→        fake_graph = {"a.ts": {"imports": set(), "importers": set()}}

  1512→    def test_setup_dep_graph_error_nonfatal(self, tmp_path):

  1528→# ── _update_review_cache robustness test ─────────────────────────
  1529→
  1530→class TestUpdateReviewCache:
  1531→    def test_cache_created_from_scratch(self, empty_state, sample_findings_data, tmp_path):
  1532→        from desloppify.review import _update_review_cache
  1533→        with patch("desloppify.review.import_findings.PROJECT_ROOT", tmp_path):
  1534→            (tmp_path / "src").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  1535→            (tmp_path / "src" / "foo.ts").write_text("content")
  1536→            (tmp_path / "src" / "bar.ts").write_text("content")
  1537→            _update_review_cache(empty_state, sample_findings_data)
  1538→        assert "review_cache" in empty_state
  1539→        assert "files" in empty_state["review_cache"]
  1540→
  1541→    def test_cache_survives_partial_review_cache(self, sample_findings_data, tmp_path):
  1542→        """If review_cache exists without files key, shouldn't crash."""
  1543→        from desloppify.review import _update_review_cache
  1544→        state = {"review_cache": {}}  # No "files" key
  1545→        with patch("desloppify.review.import_findings.PROJECT_ROOT", tmp_path):
  1546→            (tmp_path / "src").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  1547→            (tmp_path / "src" / "foo.ts").write_text("content")
  1548→            (tmp_path / "src" / "bar.ts").write_text("content")
  1549→            _update_review_cache(state, sample_findings_data)
  1550→        assert "files" in state["review_cache"]
  1551→
  1552→    def test_file_finder_called_once_in_prepare(self, mock_lang, empty_state, tmp_path):
  1553→        """prepare_review should call file_finder exactly once."""
  1554→        from desloppify.review import prepare_review
  1555→        f = tmp_path / "foo.ts"
  1556→        f.write_text("export function getData() { return 42; }\n" * 25)
  1557→        mock_lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=[str(f)])
  1558→
  1559→        prepare_review(tmp_path, mock_lang, empty_state)
  1560→        # file_finder should be called exactly once (by prepare_review itself)
  1561→        assert mock_lang.file_finder.call_count == 1

  1566→class TestSkippedFindings:
  1567→    """Findings missing required fields are tracked and reported."""
  1568→
  1569→    def test_per_file_skipped_missing_fields(self):
  1570→        from desloppify.state import _empty_state
  1571→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
  1572→        state = _empty_state()
  1573→        data = {
  1574→            "findings": [
  1575→                # Valid finding
  1576→                {"file": "src/a.ts", "dimension": "naming_quality",
  1577→                 "identifier": "x", "summary": "bad", "confidence": "high"},
  1578→                # Missing 'identifier'
  1579→                {"file": "src/b.ts", "dimension": "naming_quality",
  1580→                 "summary": "bad", "confidence": "high"},
  1581→                # Missing 'confidence'
  1582→                {"file": "src/c.ts", "dimension": "naming_quality",
  1583→                 "identifier": "y", "summary": "bad"},
  1584→            ],
  1585→        }
  1586→        diff = import_review_findings(data, state, "typescript")
  1587→        assert diff["new"] == 1
  1588→        assert diff["skipped"] == 2
  1589→        assert len(diff["skipped_details"]) == 2
  1590→        assert "identifier" in diff["skipped_details"][0]["missing"]
  1591→        assert "confidence" in diff["skipped_details"][1]["missing"]
  1592→
  1593→    def test_per_file_invalid_dimension_skipped(self):

  1598→            "findings": [

  1600→                 "identifier": "x", "summary": "bad", "confidence": "high"},
  1601→            ],
  1602→        }
  1603→        diff = import_review_findings(data, state, "typescript")
  1604→        assert diff["new"] == 0
  1605→        assert diff["skipped"] == 1
  1606→        assert "invalid dimension" in diff["skipped_details"][0]["missing"][0]

  1642→# ── Auto-resolve on re-import tests ──────────────────────────────
  1643→
  1644→class TestAutoResolveOnReImport:
  1645→    """Old findings should auto-resolve when re-imported without them."""
  1646→
  1647→    def test_holistic_auto_resolve_on_reimport(self):
  1648→        from desloppify.state import _empty_state
  1649→        from desloppify.review import import_holistic_findings
  1650→        state = _empty_state()
  1651→
  1652→        # First import: 2 holistic findings
  1653→        data1 = {
  1654→            "findings": [
  1655→                {"dimension": "cross_module_architecture", "identifier": "god_mod",
  1656→                 "summary": "too central", "confidence": "high"},
  1657→                {"dimension": "abstraction_fitness", "identifier": "util_dump",
  1658→                 "summary": "dumping ground", "confidence": "medium"},
  1659→            ],
  1660→        }
  1661→        diff1 = import_holistic_findings(data1, state, "typescript")
  1662→        assert diff1["new"] == 2
  1663→        open_ids = [fid for fid, f in state["findings"].items()
  1664→                    if f["status"] == "open"]
  1665→        assert len(open_ids) == 2
  1666→
  1667→        # Second import: only 1 finding (different from first)
  1668→        data2 = {
  1669→            "findings": [
  1670→                {"dimension": "error_consistency", "identifier": "mixed_errors",
  1671→                 "summary": "mixed strategies", "confidence": "high"},
  1672→            ],
  1673→        }
  1674→        diff2 = import_holistic_findings(data2, state, "typescript")
  1675→        assert diff2["new"] == 1
  1676→        # The 2 old findings should be auto-resolved
  1677→        assert diff2["auto_resolved"] >= 2
  1678→        still_open = [fid for fid, f in state["findings"].items()
  1679→                      if f["status"] == "open"]
  1680→        assert len(still_open) == 1
  1681→
  1682→    def test_per_file_auto_resolve_on_reimport(self):
  1683→        from desloppify.state import _empty_state
  1684→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings
  1685→        state = _empty_state()
  1686→
  1687→        # First import: findings for src/a.ts
  1688→        data1 = {
  1689→            "findings": [
  1690→                {"file": "src/a.ts", "dimension": "naming_quality",
  1691→                 "identifier": "x", "summary": "bad name", "confidence": "high"},
  1692→                {"file": "src/a.ts", "dimension": "comment_quality",
  1693→                 "identifier": "y", "summary": "stale comment", "confidence": "medium"},
  1694→            ],
  1695→        }
  1696→        diff1 = import_review_findings(data1, state, "typescript")
  1697→        assert diff1["new"] == 2
  1698→
  1699→        # Second import: re-review src/a.ts but only 1 finding remains
  1700→        data2 = {
  1701→            "findings": [
  1702→                {"file": "src/a.ts", "dimension": "naming_quality",
  1703→                 "identifier": "x", "summary": "bad name", "confidence": "high"},
  1704→            ],
  1705→        }
  1706→        diff2 = import_review_findings(data2, state, "typescript")
  1707→        # The comment_quality finding should be auto-resolved
  1708→        resolved = [f for f in state["findings"].values()
  1709→                    if f["status"] == "auto_resolved"
  1710→                    and "not reported in latest per-file" in (f.get("note") or "")]
  1711→        assert len(resolved) >= 1
  1712→
  1713→    def test_holistic_does_not_resolve_per_file(self):
  1714→        """Holistic re-import should not touch per-file review findings."""
  1715→        from desloppify.state import _empty_state
  1716→        from desloppify.review import import_review_findings, import_holistic_findings
  1717→        state = _empty_state()
  1718→
  1719→        # Import per-file findings
  1720→        per_file = {
  1721→            "findings": [
  1722→                {"file": "src/a.ts", "dimension": "naming_quality",
  1723→                 "identifier": "x", "summary": "bad name", "confidence": "high"},
  1724→            ],
  1725→        }
  1726→        import_review_findings(per_file, state, "typescript")
  1727→        per_file_ids = [fid for fid, f in state["findings"].items()
  1728→                        if f["status"] == "open"]
  1729→        assert len(per_file_ids) == 1
  1730→
  1731→        # Import holistic findings (empty) — should NOT resolve per-file
  1732→        holistic = {"findings": []}
  1733→        import_holistic_findings(holistic, state, "typescript")
  1734→        # Per-file finding should still be open
  1735→        assert state["findings"][per_file_ids[0]]["status"] == "open"
  1736→

    74→class TestSubmoduleImports:
    75→    """Each submodule can be imported independently."""
    76→
    77→    @pytest.mark.parametrize("module", [
    78→        "desloppify.review.dimensions",
    79→        "desloppify.review.context",
    80→        "desloppify.review.selection",
    81→        "desloppify.review.prepare",
    82→        "desloppify.review.import_findings",
    83→        "desloppify.review.remediation",
    84→    ])
    85→    def test_submodule_importable(self, module):
    86→        mod = importlib.import_module(module)
    87→        assert mod is not None
    88→
    89→    def test_no_circular_import(self):
    90→        """Fresh import of desloppify.review succeeds without circular import errors."""
    91→        # Remove cached modules to force fresh import
    92→        to_remove = [k for k in sys.modules if k.startswith("desloppify.review")]
    93→        removed = {}
    94→        for k in to_remove:
    95→            removed[k] = sys.modules.pop(k)
    96→        try:
    97→            import desloppify.review
    98→            # If we get here, no circular import
    99→            assert hasattr(desloppify.review, "__all__")
   100→        finally:
   101→            # Restore removed modules
   102→            sys.modules.update(removed)
   103→

    38→class TestGatherAiDebtSignals:
    39→    """Tests for _gather_ai_debt_signals."""
    40→
    41→    def test_high_comment_ratio_detected(self):
    42→        """Files with >30% comment lines should be flagged."""
    43→        # 4 comment lines out of 5 total = 80%
    44→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
    45→            # comment 1
    46→            # comment 2
    47→            # comment 3
    48→            # comment 4
    49→            x = 1
    50→        """)
    51→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals({"/src/chatty.py": content})
    52→        assert "chatty.py" in result["file_signals"]
    53→        sig = result["file_signals"]["chatty.py"]
    54→        assert "comment_ratio" in sig
    55→        assert sig["comment_ratio"] > 0.3
    56→
    57→    def test_comment_ratio_not_flagged_when_low(self):
    58→        """Files with <=30% comments should NOT be flagged for comment_ratio."""
    59→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
    60→            x = 1
    61→            y = 2
    62→            z = 3
    63→            # one comment
    64→        """)
    65→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals({"/src/normal.py": content})
    66→        signals = result["file_signals"]
    67→        if "normal.py" in signals:
    68→            assert "comment_ratio" not in signals["normal.py"]
    69→
    70→    def test_log_density_detected(self):
    71→        """Files with >3 logs per function should be flagged."""
    72→        # 1 function, 4 log calls => density 4.0
    73→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
    74→            def process():
    75→                console.log("start")
    76→                console.log("mid 1")
    77→                console.log("mid 2")
    78→                console.log("end")
    79→                return True
    80→        """)
    81→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals({"/src/noisy.py": content})
    82→        assert "noisy.py" in result["file_signals"]
    83→        sig = result["file_signals"]["noisy.py"]
    84→        assert "log_density" in sig
    85→        assert sig["log_density"] > 3.0
    86→

   179→        files = {
   180→            "/src/a.py": "# comment\nx = 1\n",         # 50%
   181→            "/src/b.py": "x = 1\ny = 2\nz = 3\nw = 4\n",  # 0%
   182→        }
   183→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals(files)
   184→        avg = result["codebase_avg_comment_ratio"]
   185→        assert 0.2 <= avg <= 0.3  # (0.5 + 0.0) / 2 = 0.25
   186→
   187→    def test_empty_content_file_skipped(self):
   188→        """A file with empty string content should be skipped (no lines)."""
   189→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals({"/src/empty.py": ""})
   190→        assert result["file_signals"] == {}
   191→
   192→    def test_multiple_signals_on_same_file(self):
   193→        """A file can have multiple signals simultaneously."""
   194→        # High comments + high log density + high guard density
   195→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   196→            # comment 1
   197→            # comment 2
   198→            # comment 3
   199→            # comment 4
   200→            function handler() {
   201→                console.log("a")
   202→                console.log("b")
   203→                console.log("c")
   204→                console.log("d")
   205→                if (x === null) return;
   206→                if (y === undefined) return;
   207→                if (z === null) return;
   208→            }
   209→        """)
   210→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals({"/src/messy.ts": content})
   211→        sig = result["file_signals"]["messy.ts"]
   212→        assert "comment_ratio" in sig
   213→        assert "log_density" in sig
   214→        assert "guard_density" in sig
   215→
   216→    def test_js_style_comments_detected(self):
   217→        """// and /* style comments should be counted."""
   218→        content = "// comment\n/* comment */\nvar x = 1;\n"
   219→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals({"/src/js.ts": content})
   220→        # 2/3 comment lines = 0.67
   221→        assert "js.ts" in result["file_signals"]
   222→        assert result["file_signals"]["js.ts"]["comment_ratio"] > 0.3
   223→
   224→    def test_star_comment_continuation_detected(self):
   225→        """Lines starting with * (JSDoc/block comment continuation) should count."""
   226→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   227→            /**
   228→             * This is a JSDoc comment
   229→             * with multiple lines
   230→             * and more lines
   231→             */
   232→            function foo() {}
   233→        """)
   234→        result = _gather_ai_debt_signals({"/src/jsdoc.ts": content})
   235→        # 5 out of 6 lines are comment-ish
   236→        assert "jsdoc.ts" in result["file_signals"]
   237→

   239→# ── Auth Context ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
   240→
   241→class TestGatherAuthContext:
   242→    """Tests for _gather_auth_context."""
   243→
   244→    def test_python_route_with_auth_detected(self):
   245→        """Python route decorator + auth decorator should be detected."""
   246→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   247→            @app.get("/users")
   248→            @login_required
   249→            def get_users():
   250→                return users
   251→
   252→            @app.post("/users")
   253→            def create_user():
   254→                return new_user
   255→        """)
   256→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/src/routes.py": content})
   257→        assert "route_auth_coverage" in result
   258→        ra = result["route_auth_coverage"]["routes.py"]
   259→        assert ra["handlers"] == 2
   260→        assert ra["with_auth"] == 1
   261→        assert ra["without_auth"] == 1
   262→
   263→    def test_ts_route_detection(self):
   264→        """TypeScript Next.js-style route handlers should be detected."""
   265→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   266→            export async function GET(req) {
   267→                return Response.json({ok: true});
   268→            }
   269→
   270→            export async function POST(req) {
   271→                const session = await getServerSession();
   272→                return Response.json({ok: true});
   273→            }
   274→        """)
   275→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/src/route.ts": content})
   276→        assert "route_auth_coverage" in result
   277→        ra = result["route_auth_coverage"]["route.ts"]
   278→        assert ra["handlers"] == 2
   279→        assert ra["with_auth"] == 1  # getServerSession counts as auth
   280→        assert ra["without_auth"] == 1
   281→
   282→    def test_express_routes_detected(self):
   283→        """Express-style app.get/post should be detected as route handlers."""
   284→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   285→            app.get("/api/data", handler);
   286→            app.post("/api/submit", handler);
   287→        """)
   288→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/src/server.ts": content})
   289→        assert "route_auth_coverage" in result
   290→        ra = result["route_auth_coverage"]["server.ts"]
   291→        assert ra["handlers"] == 2
   292→
   293→    def test_rls_table_coverage_with_and_without(self):
   294→        """SQL files should track CREATE TABLE vs ENABLE RLS."""
   295→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   296→            CREATE TABLE users (id serial primary key);
   297→            CREATE TABLE posts (id serial primary key);
   298→            CREATE TABLE comments (id serial primary key);
   299→
   300→            ALTER TABLE users ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
   301→            CREATE POLICY read_own ON posts;
   302→        """)
   303→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/migrations/001.sql": content})
   304→        assert "rls_coverage" in result
   305→        rls = result["rls_coverage"]
   306→        assert "users" in rls["with_rls"]
   307→        assert "posts" in rls["with_rls"]
   308→        assert "comments" in rls["without_rls"]
   309→
   310→    def test_rls_case_insensitive(self):
   311→        """RLS detection should be case-insensitive."""
   312→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   313→            create table my_table (id int);
   314→            alter table my_table enable row level security;
   315→        """)
   316→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/sql/schema.sql": content})
   317→        assert "rls_coverage" in result
   318→        assert "my_table" in result["rls_coverage"]["with_rls"]
   319→
   320→    def test_rls_if_not_exists(self):
   321→        """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS should be detected."""

   324→        assert "rls_coverage" in result

   327→    def test_service_role_detection(self):

   330→            const client = createClient(url, key);
   331→            const admin = createClient(url, service_role);
   332→        """)

   334→        assert "service_role_usage" in result

   336→
   337→    def test_service_role_without_create_client_not_flagged(self):

   340→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/src/config.ts": content})
   341→        assert "service_role_usage" not in result
   342→
   343→    def test_service_role_variants(self):
   344→        """All service_role naming variants should be detected."""

   346→            content = f"const x = createClient(url, {variant});\n"

   350→    def test_auth_pattern_counting(self):
   351→        """Auth pattern count should reflect number of auth checks in file."""
   352→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   353→            const session = getServerSession();
   354→            if (!session.user) return;
   355→            const user = request.user;
   356→        """)
   357→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/src/check.ts": content})
   358→        assert "auth_patterns" in result
   359→        assert result["auth_patterns"]["check.ts"] >= 2
   360→
   361→    def test_auth_decorator_patterns(self):
   362→        """Various auth decorators should be counted."""

   364→            @require_auth
   365→            def endpoint1(): pass
   366→
   367→            @auth_required
   368→            def endpoint2(): pass
   369→
   370→            @requires_auth
   371→            def endpoint3(): pass
   372→        """)
   373→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/src/api.py": content})
   374→        assert "auth_patterns" in result

   377→    def test_empty_input_returns_empty(self):

   382→    def test_no_routes_no_route_auth_key(self):

   385→        result = _gather_auth_context({"/src/util.py": content})

   388→    def test_with_auth_capped_to_handler_count(self):
   389→        """with_auth should never exceed handler count even if auth checks > routes."""
   390→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   391→            @app.get("/one")
   392→            @login_required
   393→            @require_auth
   394→            @auth_required
   395→            def endpoint():
   396→                session = getServerSession()
   397→                return request.user

   404→    def test_router_decorator_detected(self):

   410→            @router.post("/items")

   415→        assert result["route_auth_coverage"]["items.py"]["handlers"] == 2

   418→# ── Migration Signals ────────────────────────────────────────────
   419→
   420→class TestGatherMigrationSignals:
   421→    """Tests for _gather_migration_signals."""
   422→
   423→    def test_deprecated_markers_counted(self):
   424→        """@deprecated, @Deprecated, and DEPRECATED should be counted."""
   425→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   426→            @deprecated
   427→            def old_func():
   428→                pass
   429→
   430→            @Deprecated
   431→            class OldClass:
   432→                pass
   433→
   434→            # DEPRECATED: don't use this
   435→            x = 1
   436→        """)
   437→        result = _gather_migration_signals({"/src/old.py": content}, "python")
   438→        assert "deprecated_markers" in result
   439→        dm = result["deprecated_markers"]
   440→        assert dm["total"] == 3
   441→        assert dm["files"]["old.py"] == 3
   442→
   443→    def test_deprecated_across_files(self):
   444→        """Deprecated counts should aggregate across files."""
   445→        files = {
   446→            "/src/a.py": "@deprecated\ndef f(): pass\n",
   447→            "/src/b.py": "DEPRECATED\nDEPRECATED\n",
   448→        }
   449→        result = _gather_migration_signals(files, "python")
   450→        dm = result["deprecated_markers"]
   451→        assert dm["total"] == 3
   452→        assert dm["files"]["a.py"] == 1
   453→        assert dm["files"]["b.py"] == 2
   454→
   455→    def test_migration_todos_detected(self):
   456→        """TODO/FIXME/HACK with migration keywords should be detected.
   457→
   458→        Note: the regex uses ``[^:\\n]*`` between the marker and keyword,
   459→        so the keyword must appear before any colon on the same line.
   460→        """
   461→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   462→            # FIXME legacy code needs removal
   463→            # TODO remove after v2 cleanup
   464→            # HACK old api workaround
   465→            # TODO: regular todo - not migration
   466→        """)
   467→        result = _gather_migration_signals({"/src/migrate.py": content}, "python")
   468→        assert "migration_todos" in result
   469→        todos = result["migration_todos"]
   470→        assert len(todos) == 3
   471→        assert all(t["file"] == "migrate.py" for t in todos)
   472→
   473→    def test_migration_todo_text_truncated(self):
   474→        """Migration TODO text should be truncated to 120 chars."""
   475→        long_text = "TODO: migrate " + "x" * 200
   476→        result = _gather_migration_signals({"/src/long.py": long_text}, "python")
   477→        if result.get("migration_todos"):
   478→            for todo in result["migration_todos"]:
   479→                assert len(todo["text"]) <= 120
   480→
   481→    def test_migration_todos_capped_at_30(self):
   482→        """At most 30 migration TODOs should be returned."""
   483→        lines = "\n".join(f"# TODO: migrate item {i}" for i in range(40))
   484→        result = _gather_migration_signals({"/src/many.py": lines}, "python")
   485→        assert len(result.get("migration_todos", [])) <= 30
   486→
   487→    def test_pattern_pairs_ts(self):
   488→        """TypeScript pattern pairs should be detected when both old and new coexist."""
   489→        content_old = textwrap.dedent("""\
   490→            const axios = require('axios');
   491→            var x = 1;
   492→        """)
   493→        content_new = textwrap.dedent("""\
   494→            import { fetch } from 'node-fetch';
   495→            const y = 2;
   496→            let z = 3;
   497→        """)
   498→        files = {"/src/old.ts": content_old, "/src/new.ts": content_new}
   499→        result = _gather_migration_signals(files, "typescript")
   500→        assert "pattern_pairs" in result
   501→        pair_names = [p["name"] for p in result["pattern_pairs"]]
   502→        assert "require\u2192import" in pair_names
   503→        assert "var\u2192let/const" in pair_names
   504→
   505→    def test_pattern_pairs_py(self):
   506→        """Python pattern pairs should detect old+new coexistence."""
   507→        files = {
   508→            "/src/old.py": "import os\nresult = os.path.join('a', 'b')\n",
   509→            "/src/new.py": "from pathlib import Path\np = Path('a') / 'b'\n",
   510→        }
   511→        result = _gather_migration_signals(files, "python")
   512→        assert "pattern_pairs" in result
   513→        pair_names = [p["name"] for p in result["pattern_pairs"]]
   514→        assert "os.path\u2192pathlib" in pair_names
   515→
   516→    def test_pattern_pairs_not_detected_when_only_old(self):
   517→        """Pattern pairs should NOT be detected when only old pattern exists."""
   518→        content = "var x = 1;\nvar y = 2;\n"
   519→        result = _gather_migration_signals({"/src/old.ts": content}, "typescript")
   520→        # var->let/const should not appear since there are no let/const
   521→        pairs = result.get("pattern_pairs", [])
   522→        pair_names = [p["name"] for p in pairs]
   523→        assert "var\u2192let/const" not in pair_names
   524→
   525→    def test_pattern_pairs_counts(self):
   526→        """Pattern pairs should report old_count and new_count."""
   527→        files = {
   528→            "/src/a.ts": "var x = 1;\n",
   529→            "/src/b.ts": "let y = 2;\n",
   530→            "/src/c.ts": "const z = 3;\n",
   531→        }
   532→        result = _gather_migration_signals(files, "typescript")
   533→        if "pattern_pairs" in result:
   534→            for pair in result["pattern_pairs"]:
   535→                if pair["name"] == "var\u2192let/const":
   536→                    assert pair["old_count"] == 1
   537→                    assert pair["new_count"] == 2
   538→
   539→    def test_mixed_extensions_detected(self):
   540→        """Files with same stem but .js and .ts should be flagged."""
   541→        files = {
   542→            "/src/utils.js": "var x = 1;",
   543→            "/src/utils.ts": "const x = 1;",
   544→            "/src/helper.tsx": "export default function() {}",
   545→            "/src/helper.jsx": "export default function() {}",
   546→        }
   547→        result = _gather_migration_signals(files, "typescript")
   548→        assert "mixed_extensions" in result
   549→        assert "utils" in result["mixed_extensions"]
   550→        assert "helper" in result["mixed_extensions"]
   551→
   552→    def test_mixed_extensions_not_flagged_for_different_stems(self):
   553→        """Different file stems should not be flagged as mixed."""

   555→            "/src/utils.js": "var x = 1;",

   557→        }
   558→        result = _gather_migration_signals(files, "typescript")

   560→
   561→    def test_mixed_extensions_ignores_py(self):

   564→            "/src/utils.py": "x = 1",
   565→            "/src/utils.ts": "const x = 1;",
   566→        }
   567→        result = _gather_migration_signals(files, "typescript")
   568→        # .py is not in the tracked set, so utils should not be flagged

   570→
   571→    def test_mixed_extensions_capped_at_20(self):
   572→        """At most 20 mixed extension stems should be returned."""
   573→        files = {}
   574→        for i in range(25):
   575→            files[f"/src/file{i}.js"] = "var x;"

   579→
   580→    def test_empty_input(self):
   581→        """Empty file_contents should return empty dict."""
   582→        result = _gather_migration_signals({}, "python")
   583→        assert result == {}
   584→
   585→    def test_no_deprecated_no_key(self):
   586→        """If no deprecated markers found, key should be absent."""

   591→    def test_hack_with_migration_keyword(self):

   598→
   599→# ── Error Strategy Classification ────────────────────────────────
   600→
   601→class TestClassifyErrorStrategy:
   602→    """Tests for _classify_error_strategy."""
   603→
   604→    def test_throw_strategy(self):
   605→        """Files predominantly using throw/raise should classify as 'throw'."""
   606→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   607→            def validate(x):
   608→                if not x:
   609→                    raise ValueError("bad")
   610→                if x < 0:
   611→                    raise TypeError("negative")
   612→        """)
   613→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) == "throw"
   614→
   615→    def test_return_null_strategy(self):
   616→        """Files predominantly returning null/None should classify as 'return_null'."""
   617→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   618→            def find(x):
   619→                if not x:
   620→                    return None
   621→                if x < 0:
   622→                    return None
   623→                return x
   624→        """)
   625→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) == "return_null"
   626→
   627→    def test_result_type_strategy(self):
   628→        """Files using Result/Either/Ok/Err should classify as 'result_type'."""
   629→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   630→            fn process() -> Result<(), Error> {
   631→                let value = Ok(42);
   632→                let other = Err("bad");
   633→                Result::Ok(value)
   634→            }
   635→        """)
   636→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) == "result_type"
   637→
   638→    def test_try_catch_strategy(self):
   639→        """Files using try/catch blocks should classify as 'try_catch'."""
   640→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   641→            function work() {
   642→                try {
   643→                    doA();
   644→                } catch(e) {}
   645→                try {
   646→                    doB();
   647→                } catch(e) {}
   648→                try {
   649→                    doC();
   650→                } catch(e) {}
   651→            }
   652→        """)
   653→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) == "try_catch"
   654→
   655→    def test_try_python_style(self):
   656→        """Python try: should also count as try_catch."""
   657→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   658→            def work():
   659→                try:
   660→                    do_a()
   661→                except:
   662→                    pass
   663→                try:
   664→                    do_b()
   665→                except:
   666→                    pass
   667→                try:
   668→                    do_c()
   669→                except:
   670→                    pass
   671→        """)
   672→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) == "try_catch"
   673→
   674→    def test_mixed_strategy(self):
   675→        """Files with no dominant strategy (none >60%) should be 'mixed'."""
   676→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   677→            def a():
   678→                raise ValueError("bad")
   679→            def b():
   680→                return None
   681→            def c():
   682→                try:
   683→                    pass
   684→                except:
   685→                    pass
   686→        """)
   687→        result = _classify_error_strategy(content)
   688→        # Each strategy has roughly 1/3, so none > 60%
   689→        assert result == "mixed"
   690→
   691→    def test_none_for_empty_content(self):
   692→        """Empty content should return None."""
   693→        assert _classify_error_strategy("") is None
   694→
   695→    def test_none_for_no_error_patterns(self):
   696→        """Content with no error patterns should return None."""
   697→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   698→            def add(a, b):
   699→                return a + b
   700→        """)
   701→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) is None
   702→
   703→    def test_throw_js_style(self):
   704→        """JS-style 'throw new Error' should count as throw."""
   705→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   706→            function validate(x) {
   707→                if (!x) throw new Error("missing");
   708→                if (x < 0) throw new TypeError("negative");
   709→                if (x > 100) throw new RangeError("too big");
   710→            }
   711→        """)
   712→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) == "throw"
   713→
   714→    def test_return_undefined(self):
   715→        """return undefined should classify as return_null."""
   716→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   717→            function find(x) {
   718→                if (!x) return undefined;
   719→                if (x < 0) return undefined;
   720→                if (x > 100) return undefined;
   721→                return x;
   722→            }
   723→        """)
   724→        assert _classify_error_strategy(content) == "return_null"
   725→
   726→    def test_dominant_threshold_boundary(self):
   727→        """At exactly 60%, strategy IS dominant (check uses strict < 0.6)."""
   728→        # 3 throws + 2 return nulls = 5 total, throw is 60% = 0.6 -> NOT < 0.6 -> dominant
   729→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   730→            def a(): raise ValueError("a")
   731→            def b(): raise ValueError("b")
   732→            def c(): raise ValueError("c")
   733→            def d(): return None
   734→            def e(): return None
   735→        """)
   736→        result = _classify_error_strategy(content)
   737→        # 3/5 = 0.6, which is NOT < 0.6, so it counts as dominant
   738→        assert result == "throw"
   739→
   740→    def test_mixed_below_60_percent(self):
   741→        """Below 60%, no strategy is dominant so result should be 'mixed'."""
   742→        # 2 throws + 2 return nulls + 1 try = 5 total, max is 2/5 = 40% < 60%
   743→        content = textwrap.dedent("""\
   744→            def a(): raise ValueError("a")
   745→            def b(): raise ValueError("b")
   746→            def c(): return None
   747→            def d(): return None
   748→            def e():
   749→                try:
   750→                    pass
   751→                except:
   752→                    pass
   753→        """)
   754→        result = _classify_error_strategy(content)
   755→        assert result == "mixed"

    40→def _make_file(tmpdir, name, lines=30, content=None):
    41→    """Create a file with content."""
    42→    p = os.path.join(tmpdir, name)
    43→    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)
    44→    with open(p, "w") as f:
    45→        if content is not None:
    46→            f.write(content)
    47→        else:
    48→            for i in range(lines):
    49→                f.write(f"def func_{i}(): pass\n")
    50→    return p
    51→
    52→
    53→def _mock_lang(files=None):
    54→    lang = MagicMock()
    55→    lang.name = "python"
    56→    lang.file_finder = MagicMock(return_value=files or [])
    57→    lang._zone_map = None
    58→    lang._dep_graph = None
    59→    lang.zone_rules = []
    60→    lang.build_dep_graph = None
    61→    return lang

   237→                "related_files": ["utils.py", "a.py"],
   238→            },
   239→            {
   240→                "dimension": "error_consistency",
   241→                "identifier": "mixed_strategies",
   242→                "summary": "Three error strategies across modules",
   243→                "confidence": "medium",
   244→                "related_files": ["handler.py", "service.py"],
   245→            },
   246→        ]
   247→
   248→        diff = import_holistic_findings(findings_data, state, "python")
   249→
   250→        assert diff["new"] == 2
   251→        assert len(state["findings"]) == 2
   252→
   253→    def test_holistic_cache_updated(self):
   254→        state = _empty_state()
   255→        findings_data = [{
   256→            "dimension": "cross_module_architecture",

   261→

   269→    def test_holistic_potential_added(self):

   283→    def test_finding_id_contains_holistic(self):

   298→# ===================================================================
   299→# Scoring: holistic multiplier
   300→# ===================================================================
   301→
   302→
   303→class TestHolisticScoring:
   304→    def _holistic_finding(self, confidence="high", status="open"):
   305→        return {
   306→            "detector": "review",
   307→            "status": status,
   308→            "confidence": confidence,
   309→            "file": ".",
   310→            "zone": "production",
   311→            "detail": {"holistic": True},
   312→        }
   313→
   314→    def _file_finding(self, confidence="high", file="src/a.py", status="open"):
   315→        return {
   316→            "detector": "review",
   317→            "status": status,
   318→            "confidence": confidence,
   319→            "file": file,
   320→            "zone": "production",
   321→            "detail": {},
   322→        }
   323→
   324→    def test_holistic_multiplier_applied(self):
   325→        findings = {"0": self._holistic_finding(confidence="high")}
   326→        rate, issues, weighted = _detector_pass_rate("review", findings, 60)
   327→
   328→        # high confidence = 1.0 * 10 = 10.0 weighted failures
   329→        assert issues == 1
   330→        assert weighted == pytest.approx(1.0 * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER)
   331→        assert rate == pytest.approx((60 - 10.0) / 60)
   332→
   333→    def test_holistic_no_per_file_cap(self):
   334→        """Multiple holistic findings are NOT capped like per-file findings."""
   335→        findings = {
   336→            "0": self._holistic_finding(confidence="high"),
   337→            "1": self._holistic_finding(confidence="medium"),
   338→        }
   339→        rate, issues, weighted = _detector_pass_rate("review", findings, 60)
   340→
   341→        # high=1.0*10=10.0, medium=0.7*10=7.0, total=17.0
   342→        expected = 1.0 * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER + 0.7 * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
   343→        assert issues == 2
   344→        assert weighted == pytest.approx(expected)
   345→
   346→    def test_mixed_holistic_and_file(self):
   347→        """Holistic and file findings score separately."""
   348→        findings = {
   349→            "0": self._holistic_finding(confidence="high"),
   350→            "1": self._file_finding(confidence="high", file="src/a.py"),
   351→            "2": self._file_finding(confidence="high", file="src/a.py"),
   352→        }
   353→        rate, issues, weighted = _detector_pass_rate("review", findings, 60)
   354→
   355→        # Holistic: 1.0*10=10.0
   356→        # File: two findings on same file → capped at 1.0
   357→        # Total: 11.0
   358→        assert issues == 3
   359→        assert weighted == pytest.approx(11.0)
   360→
   361→    def test_holistic_resolved_not_counted(self):
   362→        findings = {"0": self._holistic_finding(confidence="high", status="fixed")}
   363→        rate, issues, weighted = _detector_pass_rate("review", findings, 60)
   364→
   365→        assert issues == 0
   366→        assert weighted == 0.0
   367→        assert rate == 1.0
   368→
   369→
   370→# ===================================================================
   371→# path_scoped_findings includes holistic
   372→# ===================================================================
   373→
   374→
   375→class TestPathScopedFindings:
   376→    def test_holistic_included_with_scan_path(self):
   377→        findings = {
   378→            "review::.::holistic::arch::abc": {
   379→                "file": ".",
   380→                "status": "open",
   381→                "detector": "review",
   382→                "detail": {"holistic": True},
   383→            },
   384→            "unused::src/a.py::foo": {
   385→                "file": "src/a.py",
   386→                "status": "open",
   387→                "detector": "unused",
   388→            },
   389→            "unused::lib/b.py::bar": {
   390→                "file": "lib/b.py",
   391→                "status": "open",
   392→                "detector": "unused",
   393→            },
   394→        }
   395→
   396→        result = path_scoped_findings(findings, "src")
   397→
   398→        # Should include holistic (file=".") and src/a.py, but not lib/b.py
   399→        assert "review::.::holistic::arch::abc" in result
   400→        assert "unused::src/a.py::foo" in result
   401→        assert "unused::lib/b.py::bar" not in result
   402→
   403→    def test_holistic_included_with_root_path(self):
   404→        findings = {
   405→            "review::.::holistic::test": {
   406→                "file": ".",
   407→                "status": "open",
   408→            },
   409→        }
   410→
   411→        result = path_scoped_findings(findings, ".")
   412→        assert len(result) == 1
   413→
   414→    def test_holistic_included_with_no_path(self):
   415→        findings = {
   416→            "review::.::holistic::test": {
   417→                "file": ".",
   418→                "status": "open",
   419→            },
   420→        }
   421→
   422→        result = path_scoped_findings(findings, None)
   423→        assert len(result) == 1

   426→# ===================================================================
   427→# Holistic staleness detection
   428→# ===================================================================
   429→
   430→
   431→class TestHolisticStaleness:
   432→    def test_no_cache_returns_unreviewed(self):
   433→        entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness({}, total_files=100)
   434→        assert len(entries) == 1
   435→        assert entries[0]["name"] == "holistic_unreviewed"
   436→
   437→    def test_fresh_cache_returns_empty(self):
   438→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   439→        cache = {
   440→            "holistic": {
   441→                "reviewed_at": now,
   442→                "file_count_at_review": 100,
   443→                "finding_count": 2,
   444→            }
   445→        }
   446→        entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness(cache, total_files=100)
   447→        assert len(entries) == 0
   448→
   449→    def test_stale_cache_returns_stale(self):
   450→        old = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(days=45)).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   451→        cache = {
   452→            "holistic": {
   453→                "reviewed_at": old,
   454→                "file_count_at_review": 100,
   455→                "finding_count": 2,
   456→            }
   457→        }
   458→        entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness(cache, total_files=100)
   459→        assert len(entries) == 1
   460→        assert entries[0]["name"] == "holistic_stale"
   461→        assert "45 days" in entries[0]["summary"]
   462→
   463→    def test_drift_returns_stale(self):
   464→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   465→        cache = {
   466→            "holistic": {
   467→                "reviewed_at": now,
   468→                "file_count_at_review": 50,
   469→                "finding_count": 2,
   470→            }
   471→        }
   472→        # 50 → 80 = 60% drift, exceeds 20% threshold
   473→        entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness(cache, total_files=80)
   474→        assert len(entries) == 1
   475→        assert entries[0]["name"] == "holistic_stale"
   476→        assert "50" in entries[0]["summary"]
   477→        assert "80" in entries[0]["summary"]
   478→
   479→    def test_small_drift_returns_empty(self):
   480→        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   481→        cache = {
   482→            "holistic": {
   483→                "reviewed_at": now,
   484→                "file_count_at_review": 100,
   485→                "finding_count": 2,
   486→            }
   487→        }
   488→        # 100 → 110 = 10% drift, within 20% threshold
   489→        entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness(cache, total_files=110)
   490→        assert len(entries) == 0
   491→
   492→    def test_unparseable_date_returns_stale(self):
   493→        cache = {
   494→            "holistic": {
   495→                "reviewed_at": "not-a-date",
   496→                "file_count_at_review": 100,
   497→                "finding_count": 2,
   498→            }
   499→        }
   500→        entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness(cache, total_files=100)
   501→        assert len(entries) == 1
   502→        assert entries[0]["name"] == "holistic_stale"
   503→

   641→# ===================================================================
   642→# Sibling behavior analysis in build_holistic_context
   643→# ===================================================================
   644→
   645→
   646→class TestSiblingBehavior:
   647→    def test_detects_outlier(self, tmp_path):
   648→        """A file missing an import shared by >60% of siblings is flagged."""
   649→        # 4 files in same dir, 3 import compute_narrative, 1 doesn't
   650→        for i in range(3):
   651→            _make_file(str(tmp_path), f"commands/cmd_{i}.py",
   652→                       content=f"from ..narrative import compute_narrative\ndef cmd_{i}(): pass\n")
   653→        _make_file(str(tmp_path), "commands/review_cmd.py",
   654→                   content="def cmd_review(): pass\n")  # Missing compute_narrative
   655→        files = [os.path.join(str(tmp_path), f"commands/{n}")
   656→                 for n in [f"cmd_{i}.py" for i in range(3)] + ["review_cmd.py"]]
   657→        lang = _mock_lang(files)
   658→        state = _empty_state()
   659→
   660→        ctx = build_holistic_context(tmp_path, lang, state, files=files)
   661→
   662→        sibling = ctx["conventions"].get("sibling_behavior", {})
   663→        assert "commands/" in sibling
   664→        outliers = sibling["commands/"]["outliers"]
   665→        outlier_files = [o["file"] for o in outliers]
   666→        assert any("review_cmd" in f for f in outlier_files)
   667→        # compute_narrative should be in shared_patterns
   668→        shared = sibling["commands/"]["shared_patterns"]
   669→        assert "compute_narrative" in shared
   670→
   671→    def test_no_outlier_when_all_share(self, tmp_path):
   672→        """No outliers when all files import the same things."""
   673→        for i in range(4):
   674→            _make_file(str(tmp_path), f"lib/mod_{i}.py",
   675→                       content="from os.path import join\ndef f(): pass\n")
   676→        files = [os.path.join(str(tmp_path), f"lib/mod_{i}.py") for i in range(4)]
   677→        lang = _mock_lang(files)
   678→        state = _empty_state()
   679→
   680→        ctx = build_holistic_context(tmp_path, lang, state, files=files)
   681→
   682→        sibling = ctx["conventions"].get("sibling_behavior", {})
   683→        # join is shared by all, no outliers
   684→        if "lib/" in sibling:
   685→            assert len(sibling["lib/"]["outliers"]) == 0
   686→
   687→    def test_too_few_siblings_skipped(self, tmp_path):
   688→        """Directories with <3 files are skipped."""
   689→        _make_file(str(tmp_path), "tiny/a.py", content="import sys\n")
   690→        _make_file(str(tmp_path), "tiny/b.py", content="import os\n")
   691→        files = [os.path.join(str(tmp_path), f"tiny/{n}.py") for n in ("a", "b")]
   692→        lang = _mock_lang(files)
   693→        state = _empty_state()
   694→
   695→        ctx = build_holistic_context(tmp_path, lang, state, files=files)
   696→
   697→        sibling = ctx["conventions"].get("sibling_behavior", {})
   698→        assert "tiny/" not in sibling
   699→

   701→# ===================================================================
   702→# File excerpts on god_modules and util_files
   703→# ===================================================================
   704→
   705→
   706→class TestExcerptsInContext:
   707→    def test_god_module_has_excerpt(self, tmp_path):
   708→        """God modules include an excerpt field."""
   709→        f1 = _make_file(str(tmp_path), "core.py", lines=50)
   710→        lang = _mock_lang([f1])
   711→        # Build a dep graph that makes f1 a god module (>=5 importers)
   712→        lang._dep_graph = {
   713→            f1: {"importers": {f"mod_{i}" for i in range(6)}, "imports": set()},
   714→        }
   715→        state = _empty_state()
   716→
   717→        ctx = build_holistic_context(tmp_path, lang, state, files=[f1])
   718→
   719→        god_mods = ctx["architecture"].get("god_modules", [])
   720→        assert len(god_mods) >= 1
   721→        assert "excerpt" in god_mods[0]
   722→        assert "def func_0" in god_mods[0]["excerpt"]
   723→
   724→    def test_util_file_has_excerpt(self, tmp_path):
   725→        f1 = _make_file(str(tmp_path), "src/utils.py", lines=50)
   726→        f2 = _make_file(str(tmp_path), "src/main.py", lines=20)
   727→        lang = _mock_lang([f1, f2])
   728→        state = _empty_state()
   729→
   730→        ctx = build_holistic_context(tmp_path, lang, state, files=[f1, f2])
   731→
   732→        util_files = ctx["abstractions"]["util_files"]
   733→        assert len(util_files) >= 1
   734→        assert "excerpt" in util_files[0]
   735→        assert "def func_0" in util_files[0]["excerpt"]
   736→

   738→# ===================================================================
   739→# _build_investigation_batches
   740→# ===================================================================
   741→
   742→
   743→class TestBuildInvestigationBatches:
   744→    def test_returns_batches_from_rich_context(self):
   745→        """Batches are built from holistic context data."""
   746→        ctx = {
   747→            "architecture": {
   748→                "god_modules": [{"file": "core.py", "importers": 10, "excerpt": "..."}],
   749→                "top_imported": {"core.py": 10},
   750→            },
   751→            "coupling": {
   752→                "module_level_io": [{"file": "init.py", "line": 5, "code": "open('f')"}],
   753→            },
   754→            "conventions": {
   755→                "sibling_behavior": {
   756→                    "commands/": {
   757→                        "shared_patterns": {"compute_narrative": {"count": 6, "total": 7}},
   758→                        "outliers": [{"file": "commands/review_cmd.py", "missing": ["compute_narrative"]}],
   759→                    }
   760→                },
   761→            },
   762→            "errors": {"strategy_by_directory": {"src/": {"try_catch": 5, "throws": 3, "returns_null": 2}}},
   763→            "abstractions": {"util_files": [{"file": "utils.py", "loc": 200, "excerpt": "..."}]},
   764→            "dependencies": {"existing_cycles": 1, "cycle_summaries": ["cycle in graph.py"]},
   765→            "testing": {"critical_untested": [{"file": "scoring.py", "importers": 8}]},
   766→            "api_surface": {"sync_async_mix": ["api.py"]},
   767→        }
   768→        lang = _mock_lang()
   769→
   770→        batches = _build_investigation_batches(ctx, lang)
   771→
   772→        assert len(batches) >= 1
   773→        names = [b["name"] for b in batches]
   774→        assert "Architecture & Coupling" in names
   775→        assert "Conventions & Errors" in names
   776→
   777→        # Check that files are populated
   778→        arch_batch = next(b for b in batches if b["name"] == "Architecture & Coupling")
   779→        assert "core.py" in arch_batch["files_to_read"]
   780→
   781→        conv_batch = next(b for b in batches if b["name"] == "Conventions & Errors")
   782→        assert "commands/review_cmd.py" in conv_batch["files_to_read"]
   783→
   784→    def test_empty_context_returns_no_batches(self):
   785→        """No batches when context has no data."""
   786→        ctx = {
   787→            "architecture": {},
   788→            "coupling": {},
   789→            "conventions": {},
   790→            "errors": {"strategy_by_directory": {}},
   791→            "abstractions": {"util_files": []},
   792→            "dependencies": {},
   793→            "testing": {},
   794→            "api_surface": {},
   795→        }
   796→        lang = _mock_lang()
   797→
   798→        batches = _build_investigation_batches(ctx, lang)
   799→

   898→def _state_with_holistic_findings(*findings_args):
   899→    """Create a state with holistic findings for plan testing."""
   900→    state = _empty_state()
   901→    state["potentials"] = {"python": {"review": HOLISTIC_POTENTIAL}}
   902→    state["objective_score"] = 45.0
   903→    state["objective_strict"] = 38.0
   904→    for fid, conf, dim, summary in findings_args:
   905→        state["findings"][fid] = {
   906→            "id": fid,
   907→            "file": ".",
   908→            "status": "open",
   909→            "detector": "review",
   910→            "confidence": conf,
   911→            "detail": {
   912→                "holistic": True,
   913→                "dimension": dim,
   914→                "related_files": ["src/a.py", "src/b.py"],
   915→                "evidence": ["evidence line 1"],
   916→                "suggestion": "do the thing",
   917→                "reasoning": "because reasons",
   918→            },
   919→            "summary": summary,
   920→        }
   921→    return state
   922→
   923→
   924→class TestGenerateRemediationPlan:
   925→    def test_basic_plan_content(self):
   926→        state = _state_with_holistic_findings(
   927→            ("review::.::holistic::arch::abc", "high", "cross_module_architecture", "God module found"),
   928→        )
   929→
   930→        plan = generate_remediation_plan(state, "python")
   931→
   932→        assert "# Holistic Review: Remediation Plan" in plan
   933→        assert "God module found" in plan
   934→        assert "cross module architecture" in plan
   935→        assert "45.0/100" in plan
   936→        assert "resolve fixed" in plan
   937→
   938→    def test_priority_ordering_by_weight(self):
   939→        state = _state_with_holistic_findings(
   940→            ("review::.::holistic::test::low1", "low", "test_strategy", "Low impact thing"),
   941→            ("review::.::holistic::arch::high1", "high", "cross_module_architecture", "High impact thing"),
   942→        )
   943→
   944→        plan = generate_remediation_plan(state, "python")
   945→
   946→        # High confidence should come first (Priority 1)
   947→        high_pos = plan.index("High impact thing")
   948→        low_pos = plan.index("Low impact thing")
   949→        assert high_pos < low_pos

    44→def _make_file(tmpdir, name, lines=30):
    45→    """Create a file with enough lines to pass _MIN_REVIEW_LOC."""
    46→    p = os.path.join(tmpdir, name)
    47→    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)
    48→    with open(p, "w") as f:
    49→        for i in range(lines):
    50→            f.write(f"line {i}\n")
    51→    return p
    52→
    53→
    54→def _hash_content(path):
    55→    with open(path, "rb") as f:
    56→        return hashlib.sha256(f.read()).hexdigest()[:16]

    62→class TestReviewCoverageNoCache:

    65→    def test_all_flagged(self, tmp_path):
    66→        f1 = _make_file(str(tmp_path), "module_a.py")
    67→        f2 = _make_file(str(tmp_path), "module_b.py")
    68→        entries, potential = detect_review_coverage(
    69→            [f1, f2], zone_map=None, review_cache={}, lang_name="python"
    70→        )

    72→        assert len(entries) == 2
    73→        assert all(e["name"] == "unreviewed" for e in entries)
    74→        assert all(e["confidence"] == "low" for e in entries)

   277→class TestIDCollision:
   278→    """Two findings same file+dimension+identifier get distinct IDs."""
   279→
   280→    def test_distinct_ids_with_evidence_lines(self):
   281→        findings_data = [
   282→            {
   283→                "file": "module.py",
   284→                "dimension": "naming_quality",
   285→                "identifier": "processData",
   286→                "summary": "Generic name — rename to reconcileInvoice",
   287→                "confidence": "high",
   288→                "evidence_lines": [15, 32],
   289→                "evidence": ["processData is vague"],
   290→                "suggestion": "rename to reconcileInvoice",
   291→            },
   292→            {
   293→                "file": "module.py",
   294→                "dimension": "naming_quality",
   295→                "identifier": "processData",
   296→                "summary": "Name/behavior mismatch — getX mutates state",
   297→                "confidence": "medium",
   298→                "evidence_lines": [45, 60],
   299→                "evidence": ["processData mutates state"],
   300→                "suggestion": "rename to updateInvoice",
   301→            },
   302→        ]
   303→        state = _empty_state()
   304→        diff = import_review_findings(findings_data, state, "python")
   305→
   306→        # Both should be present with distinct IDs (content-hash disambiguated)
   307→        ids = list(state["findings"].keys())
   308→        assert len(ids) == 2
   309→        assert ids[0] != ids[1]
   310→

   341→    def test_same_finding_same_id(self):

   467→class TestHolisticStalenessInCoverage:
   468→    """Holistic staleness entries emitted by detect_holistic_review_staleness."""
   469→
   470→    def test_no_holistic_cache_emits_unreviewed(self):
   471→        from desloppify.detectors.review_coverage import detect_holistic_review_staleness
   472→        entries = detect_holistic_review_staleness({}, total_files=50)
   473→        assert len(entries) == 1

   478→    def test_fresh_holistic_emits_nothing(self):
   479→        from desloppify.detectors.review_coverage import detect_holistic_review_staleness

   492→
   493→    def test_drifted_file_count_emits_stale(self):

   504→# max_age_days=0 ("never") behavior
